| `compute_full_tree` | 'auto' | 'auto' | 'auto' or bool, default 'auto' | {'auto'} \| boolean |
| `linkage` | 'single' | 'ward' | {'single', 'complete', 'average', 'ward'}, default 'single' | {'average', 'complete', 'single', 'ward'} |

| method_extra | `predict` | yes |  | cuML-style predict for new points; scikit-learn has fit_predict only. |
| method_extra | `receipt` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |

## GradientBoostingRegressor

`mojolearn.GradientBoostingRegressor` (`_gbdt_adapters.py`) and `sklearn.ensemble.GradientBoostingRegressor`.

| parameter | Mojolearn default | scikit-learn default | Mojolearn accepts | scikit-learn accepts |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `loss` | absent | 'squared_error' |  | {'absolute_error', 'huber', 'quantile', 'squared_error'} |

| method_extra | `check` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `load` | yes |  | Mojolearn's native model archive (a file format read by the compiled loader on every vendor and the CPU). scikit-learn models are pickled instead; Mojolearn models pickle too. |
| method_extra | `receipt` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |

| `max_cluster_size` | 0 | None | int, default 0 | None \| Integral in [1, inf) |
| `metric` | 'euclidean' | 'euclidean' | {'euclidean'}, default 'euclidean' | {'braycurtis', 'canberra', 'chebyshev', 'cityblock', 'correlation', 'cosine', 'dice', 'euclidean', 'hamming', 'haversine', 'infinity', 'jaccard', 'l1', 'l2', 'mahalanobis', 'manhattan', 'matching', 'minkowski', 'nan_euclidean', 'p', 'precomputed', 'pyfunc', 'rogerstanimoto', 'russellrao', 'seuclidean', 'sokalmichener', 'sokalsneath', 'sqeuclidean', 'wminkowski', 'yule'} \| builtin_function_or_method |

| `algorithm` | absent | 'auto' |  | {'auto', 'ball_tree', 'brute', 'kd_tree'} |
| `leaf_size` | absent | 40 |  | Integral in [1, inf) |
| `n_jobs` | absent | None |  | Integral \| None |
| `cluster_selection_method` | 'eom' | 'eom' | {'eom', 'leaf'}, default 'eom' | {'eom', 'leaf'} |
| `allow_single_cluster` | False | False | bool, default False | boolean |
| `store_centers` | absent | None |  | None \| {'both', 'centroid', 'medoid'} |
| `copy` | absent | 'warn' |  | boolean \| Hidden |

| difference | name | Mojolearn | scikit-learn | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| default | `max_cluster_size` | 0 | None | 0 means no limit (cuML's convention); scikit-learn's None means the same. |

| param_missing | `leaf_size` |  | 40 | UNCLASSIFIED |
| param_missing | `n_jobs` |  | None | UNCLASSIFIED |
| param_missing | `store_centers` |  | None | Not implemented: one GPU route (no tree choice), no centroid/medoid storage; X is never modified, but scikit-learn 1.9's copy='warn' deprecation is not mirrored. |
| param_missing | `copy` |  | 'warn' | Not implemented: one GPU route (no tree choice), no centroid/medoid storage; X is never modified, but scikit-learn 1.9's copy='warn' deprecation is not mirrored. |
| param_extra | `prediction_data` | False |  | cuML's switch that keeps the data approximate_predict needs. |
| param_extra | `numeric_mode` | None |  | Mojolearn's numeric contract: 'identical' (the same bits on Apple, NVIDIA and AMD GPUs) or 'fast' (Apple only). scikit-learn has no such choice. |
| param_extra | `device` | None |  | Mojolearn places the computation on a device ('gpu' or 'cpu'); scikit-learn runs on the host only. |

| difference | name | Mojolearn | scikit-learn | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| default | `penalty` | 'l2' | 'deprecated' | scikit-learn 1.9's default is a deprecation or warning sentinel; Mojolearn's default is the explicit value the sentinel stands for (or the only value supported). |
| default | `l1_ratio` | None | 0.0 | None means no elastic-net share (only used with penalty='elasticnet'); scikit-learn 1.9 moved to l1_ratio=0.0 with the penalty argument deprecated. Same model at the defaults. |
| param_missing | `dual` |  | False | The GPU quasi-Newton solver works on the primal only and fits the intercept as its own unpenalized weight (penalized_intercept=True penalizes it), so liblinear's dual, intercept_scaling and seed have no meaning. |
| param_missing | `intercept_scaling` |  | 1 | The GPU quasi-Newton solver works on the primal only and fits the intercept as its own unpenalized weight (penalized_intercept=True penalizes it), so liblinear's dual, intercept_scaling and seed have no meaning. |
| param_missing | `random_state` |  | None | The GPU quasi-Newton solver works on the primal only and fits the intercept as its own unpenalized weight (penalized_intercept=True penalizes it), so liblinear's dual, intercept_scaling and seed have no meaning. |
| default | `solver` | 'qn' | 'lbfgs' | Only 'qn' (the GPU quasi-Newton solver, cuML's name) exists; scikit-learn's 'lbfgs' is a CPU SciPy solver. |
| default | `max_iter` | 1000 | 100 | cuML's default for the float32 quasi-Newton solver. Changing it changes results on unconverged fits: an A/B, not a rename. |
| param_missing | `verbose` |  | 0 | Its constructor is one call into the compiled classical owner, which stores exactly its declared fields; accepting a no-op keyword needs that native constructor to take it (Mojo work, not a rename). X is never modified and the work runs on the device. |
| param_missing | `warm_start` |  | False | Warm starts are not implemented in the native fit; it needs kernel and state work, not a rename. |
| param_missing | `n_jobs` |  | None | Its constructor is one call into the compiled classical owner, which stores exactly its declared fields; accepting a no-op keyword needs that native constructor to take it (Mojo work, not a rename). X is never modified and the work runs on the device. |
| param_extra | `linesearch_max_iter` | 50 |  | Controls of the GPU quasi-Newton solver, in cuML's names. |
| param_extra | `numeric_mode` | None |  | Mojolearn's numeric contract: 'identical' (the same bits on Apple, NVIDIA and AMD GPUs) or 'fast' (Apple only). scikit-learn has no such choice. |
| param_extra | `device` | None |  | Mojolearn places the computation on a device ('gpu' or 'cpu'); scikit-learn runs on the host only. |
| method_missing | `densify` |  | yes | coef_ is always a dense float32 device result; there is no sparse coefficient storage to convert to or from. |
| method_missing | `set_callbacks` |  | yes | scikit-learn 1.9's callback API calls user Python during fit; Mojolearn never calls back into Python during an operation (AGENTS.md, no calls back into Python). |
| method_missing | `sparsify` |  | yes | coef_ is always a dense float32 device result; there is no sparse coefficient storage to convert to or from. |
| method_extra | `check` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `load` | yes |  | Mojolearn's native model archive (a file format read by the compiled loader on every vendor and the CPU). scikit-learn models are pickled instead; Mojolearn models pickle too. |
| method_extra | `numeric_mode_used` | yes |  | Reports which GPU vendor and numeric mode the last call ran on; part of the identity contract. |
| method_extra | `receipt` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `save` | yes |  | Mojolearn's native model archive (a file format read by the compiled loader on every vendor and the CPU). scikit-learn models are pickled instead; Mojolearn models pickle too. |
| method_extra | `vendor_used` | yes |  | Reports which GPU vendor and numeric mode the last call ran on; part of the identity contract. |
| attribute_missing | `feature_names_in_` |  | yes | Mojolearn reads X through the buffer protocol and keeps no column names; storing a DataFrame's names is UNPACK/PACK work in the compiled boundary, not a rename. |
| attribute_extra | `input_copied_` | yes |  | Whether fit had to copy X to float32 C order (the kernels read float32 C-contiguous buffers without a copy). |
| attribute_extra | `objective_` | yes |  | Mojolearn-only fitted attribute (cuML-compatible or a solver report), documented in the class docstring. |
| attribute_extra | `retcode_` | yes |  | Mojolearn-only fitted attribute (cuML-compatible or a solver report), documented in the class docstring. |

| `gamma` | 'auto' | 'scale' | float, 'auto' or 'scale', default 'auto' | {'auto', 'scale'} \| Real in [0.0, inf) |

| `shrinking` | absent | True |  | boolean |

| `nochange_steps` | 1000 | absent | int, default 1000 |  |

| difference | name | Mojolearn | scikit-learn | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| default | `gamma` | 'auto' | 'scale' | 'auto' is cuML's default; 'scale' (scikit-learn's) is accepted. Changing the default changes every kernel fit: an A/B, not a rename. |
| param_missing | `shrinking` |  | True | The GPU SMO solver has no shrinking heuristic; scikit-learn's shrinking changes the iteration path, so accepting and ignoring it would not be honest. |
| default | `probability` | False | 'deprecated' | scikit-learn 1.9's default is a deprecation or warning sentinel; Mojolearn's default is the explicit value the sentinel stands for (or the only value supported). |
| default | `cache_size` | 1024.0 | 200 | Here it sizes the prediction kernel tile (results do not depend on it); scikit-learn's is libsvm's training cache in MiB. |
| param_extra | `nochange_steps` | 1000 |  | cuML's SMO stopping control. |
| param_extra | `output_type` | None |  | cuML's output type switch; only None is accepted (results are NumPy or mojolearn.Array). |
| param_extra | `numeric_mode` | None |  | Mojolearn's numeric contract: 'identical' (the same bits on Apple, NVIDIA and AMD GPUs) or 'fast' (Apple only). scikit-learn has no such choice. |
| method_extra | `check` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `load` | yes |  | Mojolearn's native model archive (a file format read by the compiled loader on every vendor and the CPU). scikit-learn models are pickled instead; Mojolearn models pickle too. |
| method_extra | `numeric_mode_used` | yes |  | Reports which GPU vendor and numeric mode the last call ran on; part of the identity contract. |
| method_extra | `receipt` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `save` | yes |  | Mojolearn's native model archive (a file format read by the compiled loader on every vendor and the CPU). scikit-learn models are pickled instead; Mojolearn models pickle too. |
| method_extra | `vendor_used` | yes |  | Reports which GPU vendor and numeric mode the last call ran on; part of the identity contract. |
| attribute_missing | `class_weight_` |  | yes | Not stored by the compiled fit; adding it is a value from the native PACK or kernel work, not a rename. |

| difference | name | Mojolearn | scikit-learn | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| default | `gamma` | 'auto' | 'scale' | 'auto' is cuML's default; 'scale' (scikit-learn's) is accepted. Changing the default changes every kernel fit: an A/B, not a rename. |
| param_missing | `shrinking` |  | True | The GPU SMO solver has no shrinking heuristic; scikit-learn's shrinking changes the iteration path, so accepting and ignoring it would not be honest. |
| default | `cache_size` | 1024.0 | 200 | Here it sizes the prediction kernel tile (results do not depend on it); scikit-learn's is libsvm's training cache in MiB. |
| param_extra | `nochange_steps` | 1000 |  | cuML's SMO stopping control. |
| param_extra | `output_type` | None |  | cuML's output type switch; only None is accepted (results are NumPy or mojolearn.Array). |
| param_extra | `numeric_mode` | None |  | Mojolearn's numeric contract: 'identical' (the same bits on Apple, NVIDIA and AMD GPUs) or 'fast' (Apple only). scikit-learn has no such choice. |
| method_extra | `check` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `load` | yes |  | Mojolearn's native model archive (a file format read by the compiled loader on every vendor and the CPU). scikit-learn models are pickled instead; Mojolearn models pickle too. |

| attribute_missing | `feature_names_in_` |  | yes | Mojolearn reads X through the buffer protocol and keeps no column names; storing a DataFrame's names is UNPACK/PACK work in the compiled boundary, not a rename. |

| attribute_extra | `numeric_mode_` | yes |  | The numeric mode the fit ran in; part of the identity contract. |

## VotingClassifier

`mojolearn.VotingClassifier` (`_expansion_trees.py`) and `sklearn.ensemble.VotingClassifier`.

| parameter | Mojolearn default | scikit-learn default | Mojolearn accepts | scikit-learn accepts |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `estimators` | <required> | <required> | list of (str, estimator) | list |
| `voting` | 'hard' | 'hard' | {"hard", "soft"}, default "hard" | {'hard', 'soft'} |
| `weights` | None | None | sequence of float, default None | array-like \| None |
| `n_jobs` | None | None | None | None \| Integral |
| `flatten_transform` | True | True | bool, default True | boolean |
| `verbose` | False | False | bool, default False | verbose |

Methods: `check`, `fit`, `fit_transform`, `get_params`, `numeric_mode_used`, `predict`, `predict_proba`, `receipt`, `score`, `set_params`, `transform`, `vendor_used`.
Fitted attributes: `classes_`, `estimators_`, `n_classes_`, `n_features_in_`, `named_estimators_`.

| difference | name | Mojolearn | scikit-learn | reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| method_missing | `get_feature_names_out` |  | yes | No feature names are carried (see feature_names_in_); building output names is PACK work in the compiled boundary, not a rename. |
| method_extra | `check` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `numeric_mode_used` | yes |  | Reports which GPU vendor and numeric mode the last call ran on; part of the identity contract. |
| method_extra | `receipt` | yes |  | Mojolearn's identity tools: `check` refits on the other device and compares the bits, `receipt` records what ran. scikit-learn has no cross-device contract. |
| method_extra | `vendor_used` | yes |  | Reports which GPU vendor and numeric mode the last call ran on; part of the identity contract. |
| attribute_missing | `feature_names_in_` |  | yes | Mojolearn reads X through the buffer protocol and keeps no column names; storing a DataFrame's names is UNPACK/PACK work in the compiled boundary, not a rename. |
| attribute_missing | `le_` |  | yes | A Python helper object inside scikit-learn's fitted model (tree structure, neighbor index, sub-estimator, interpolator); Mojolearn keeps that state natively and exposes no such object. |
| attribute_extra | `n_classes_` | yes |  | Mojolearn-only fitted attribute (cuML-compatible or a solver report), documented in the class docstring. |
